However, this object is later removed during cleanup because it matches the default empty object pattern, and no flag (`_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom`) was set to preserve it. The bug occurs specifically for dashboards where the frontend's panel defaults injection creates the `custom` object after v38 runs, causing field overrides to be silently dropped.\n\n## Root Cause\n\n### Primary Issue: Premature Custom Object Creation and Loss\n\n**Location:** `/workspace/apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38.go:120-123`\n\n```go\n// Line 120-123: V38 assumes custom object exists; if not, silently skips processing\ncustom, ok := defaults[\"custom\"].(map[string]interface{})\nif !ok {\n    continue  // REGRESSION: Skips table processing if custom is absent\n}\n```\n\n**The Problem:**\nThe v38 migration checks if `fieldConfig.defaults.custom` exists **after** panel defaults have been applied (line 75 in `migrate.go`). However, the order of operations creates a timing issue:\n\n1. **Line 67-71 (migrate.go):** `trackOriginalFieldConfigCustom()` runs and records which panels had `custom` in the **original** input\n2. **Line 75 (migrate.go):** `applyFrontendDefaults()` is called\n3. **Line 79-85 (migrate.go):** `applyPanelDefaults()` creates `fieldConfig.defaults` and `fieldConfig.overrides` but **does NOT create `custom`**\n4. **Line 119-127 (migrate.go):** Schema migrations run, including v38\n5. **Line 135 (migrate.go):** `cleanupDashboardForSave()` runs, which includes cleanup logic\n\n**Critical Gap:** When a dashboard doesn't have `fieldConfig.defaults.custom` in the original JSON:\n- `_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom` flag is NOT set (line 1155-1157 in `frontend_defaults.go`)\n- `applyPanelDefaults()` creates empty `fieldConfig.defaults` but no `custom` object\n- v38 checks for `custom` existence and **skips the table entirely** if missing\n\n### Secondary Issue: Cleanup Removes Migrated Field Overrides\n\n**Location:** `/workspace/apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/frontend_defaults.go:606-620`\n\nEven if v38 created field overrides, the cleanup phase would remove them:\n\n```go\n// Line 606-620: Special handling for fieldConfig during cleanup\nif prop == \"fieldConfig\" {\n    // Check if we need to preserve the custom object before removing fieldConfig\n    if panel[\"_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom\"] == true {\n        // Only preserves if original input HAD custom\n        // ...\n    }\n    delete(panel, prop)  // Removes fieldConfig if matches defaults\n}\n```\n\nSince dashboards without explicit `custom` don't have the `_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom` flag, the entire `fieldConfig` can be removed, along with any field overrides.\n\n## Evidence\n\n### Code References\n\n**V38 Migration Function (v38.go:75-86):**\n```go\nfunc V38(_ context.Context, dashboard map[string]interface{}) error {\n    dashboard[\"schemaVersion\"] = int(38)\n\n    panels, ok := dashboard[\"panels\"].([]interface{})\n    if !ok {\n        return nil\n    }\n\n    // Process all panels, including nested ones\n    processPanelsV38(panels)\n\n    return nil\n}\n```\n\n**ProcessPanelsV38 - The Critical Check (v38.go:90-137):**\n```go\nfunc processPanelsV38(panels []interface{}) {\n    for _, panel := range panels {\n        p, ok := panel.(map[string]interface{})\n        if !ok {\n            continue\n        }\n\n        // ... row recursion ...\n\n        // Only process table panels\n        if p[\"type\"] != \"table\" {\n            continue\n        }\n\n        fieldConfig, ok := p[\"fieldConfig\"].(map[string]interface{})\n        if !ok {\n            continue\n        }\n\n        defaults, ok := fieldConfig[\"defaults\"].(map[string]interface{})\n        if !ok {\n            continue\n        }\n\n        custom, ok := defaults[\"custom\"].(map[string]interface{})\n        if !ok {\n            continue  // ← REGRESSION HERE: skips table if custom doesn't exist\n        }\n\n        // Migration only happens if custom exists\n        if displayMode, exists := custom[\"displayMode\"]; exists {\n            // ... migration logic ...\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n**Original Tracking (frontend_defaults.go:1151-1159):**\n```go\nfunc trackPanelOriginalFieldConfigCustom(panel map[string]interface{}) {\n    // Mark if this panel had fieldConfig.defaults.custom in original input\n    if fieldConfig, ok := panel[\"fieldConfig\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n        if defaults, ok := fieldConfig[\"defaults\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n            if _, hasCustom := defaults[\"custom\"]; hasCustom {\n                panel[\"_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom\"] = true  // Only set if custom existed\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // ...\n}\n```\n\n**Cleanup Logic (frontend_defaults.go:606-620):**\n```go\nif prop == \"fieldConfig\" {\n    // Check if we need to preserve the custom object before removing fieldConfig\n    if panel[\"_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom\"] == true {\n        // Ensure fieldConfig structure exists with custom object\n        if fieldConfig, ok := panelValue.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n            if defaults, ok := fieldConfig[\"defaults\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n                if _, hasCustom := defaults[\"custom\"]; !hasCustom {\n                    defaults[\"custom\"] = map[string]interface{}{}\n                }\n                // Don't remove fieldConfig if we added custom back\n                continue\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    delete(panel, prop)  // Removes fieldConfig (and any overrides)\n}\n```\n\n**Panel Defaults Application (frontend_defaults.go:102-117):**\n```go\nif _, exists := panel[\"fieldConfig\"]; !exists {\n    panel[\"fieldConfig\"] = map[string]interface{}{\n        \"defaults\":  map[string]interface{}{},  // ← Empty defaults, NO custom\n        \"overrides\": []interface{}{},\n    }\n} else {\n    // Add missing defaults and overrides\n    if fieldConfig, ok := panel[\"fieldConfig\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n        if _, hasDefaults := fieldConfig[\"defaults\"]; !hasDefaults {\n            fieldConfig[\"defaults\"] = map[string]interface{}{}  // ← No custom\n        }\n        if _, hasOverrides := fieldConfig[\"overrides\"]; !hasOverrides {\n            fieldConfig[\"overrides\"] = []interface{}{}\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Affected Components\n\n### Migration Pipeline Files\n\n1. **`/workspace/apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38.go`** (Lines 90-137)\n   - `processPanelsV38()`: Assumes `custom` object exists, skips tables if missing\n   - `migrateOverrides()`: Only runs if table processing succeeds\n\n2. **`/workspace/apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/frontend_defaults.go`** (Lines 557-662)\n   - `filterDefaultValues()`: Removes fieldConfig if matches default (lines 606-620)\n   - `trackPanelOriginalFieldConfigCustom()`: Only flags panels with original custom (lines 1151-1169)\n   - `applyPanelDefaults()`: Creates defaults but not custom (lines 102-117)\n\n3. **`/workspace/apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/migrate.go`** (Lines 51-142)\n   - `Migrate()`: Controls execution order of tracking, defaults, and cleanup\n\n### Affected Panel Types\n\n- **Table panels** with field overrides that reference `custom.displayMode`\n- **Only dashboards** with schema version < 38 and NO explicit `fieldConfig.defaults.custom`\n\n### Migration Chain Trigger\n\n- Affects dashboards imported from v10.3 or earlier that are being migrated to v10.4+ (schema v38+)\n- Specifically those with table panels that have field overrides but no explicit `custom` config in the original JSON\n\n## Why Dashboards with Explicit `defaults.custom` Are Unaffected\n\nDashboards that explicitly include `fieldConfig.defaults.custom` in their saved JSON work correctly because:\n\n1. **Original tracking succeeds** (line 1155-1157): `_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom = true` is set\n2. **V38 migration executes** (line 120): `custom` object exists, so type assertion succeeds\n3. **Field overrides are migrated** (line 135-179): `custom.displayMode` → `custom.cellOptions`\n4. **Cleanup preserves the structure** (line 655-659): Because the flag is set, `fieldConfig` is not removed\n\nThe protection is only in place for panels that **originally had** the `custom` object in the JSON.\n\n## Affected Dashboard Schema Version\n\n- **Triggers on:** Dashboard schema version < 38 (v10.3 and earlier)\n- **Fixed by:** Schema version 38 migration (intended fix)\n- **Bug manifests:** When v38 migration runs on dashboards lacking original `fieldConfig.defaults.custom`\n\n## Specific Conditional Logic Failure\n\n**The exact failing condition (v38.go:120-123):**\n\n```go\n// This condition fails when:\n// 1. Panel is a table\n// 2. fieldConfig.defaults exists (created by applyPanelDefaults)\n// 3. fieldConfig.defaults.custom does NOT exist (not in original JSON, not created by defaults)\n\ncustom, ok := defaults[\"custom\"].(map[string]interface{})\nif !ok {\n    continue  // Silent skip - no migration happens, no error is raised\n}\n```\n\n**Why it's a regression:**\n- The frontend's DashboardModel and PanelModel constructors **do create** default structures during UI rendering\n- The migration assumes these structures already exist before v38 runs\n- But `applyPanelDefaults()` doesn't create the `custom` object, only `defaults` and `overrides`\n- This creates a **mismatch between what the migration expects and what frontend defaults provide**\n\n## Recommendation\n\nThe fix requires one of these strategies:\n\n1. **Option A (Preferred - Matches Frontend Behavior):** Modify `applyPanelDefaults()` to create `fieldConfig.defaults.custom` as an empty object for all panels, matching what the frontend PanelModel constructor does. This ensures v38 always has the `custom` object to work with.\n\n2. **Option B (Backward-Compatible):** Modify v38's `processPanelsV38()` to create the `custom` object if it doesn't exist before processing:\n   ```go\n   if _, ok := defaults[\"custom\"]; !ok {\n       defaults[\"custom\"] = map[string]interface{}{}\n   }\n   // Then proceed with migration\n   ```\n\n3. **Option C (Tracking-Based):** Modify the tracking logic to mark all tables as needing migration, not just those with explicit `custom`, and handle the object creation during v38 cleanup phase.\n\n**Key Insight:** The root cause is the gap between when the tracking (`trackOriginalFieldConfigCustom`) occurs and when the panel defaults (`applyPanelDefaults`) are applied. The `custom` object is neither tracked nor created before v38 runs, causing the migration to silently skip tables that should be migrated.\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":353,"cache_read_input_tokens":46313,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":353,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d47f9616-8a41-4cee-a1b6-78f4486ee8dc","uuid":"de9fc10d-ba40-474f-83b0-625ad82ed170"}
